Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the genetics structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 692. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 4 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by genetics generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 692, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 4. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the genetics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 825. The moduli space has dimension at most 361 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on genetics structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 825. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 361. QED.
